[P-001]","completion":null,"hasCompletionEvidence":false},{"ok":true,"id":"WI-6554700010011203","availability":"available","workItem":{"id":"WI-6554700010011203","kind":"bug","family":"issue","harness":"papercusp","title":"DB backup is STALE: pg/papercusp-transcript is 47h old (>= 6h threshold) — we may have no recoverable database","summary":"EI-10733 durable backup-health probe: '/mnt/backup/db-dumps/pg/papercusp-transcript' has not advanced in 47 hours (size 4096B). The DB dump is supposed to run ~hourly.\n\nTHIS IS THE SIGNAL THAT WAS MISSING. On 2026-07-12 the backup had been failing 100% for ~30 DAYS (newest good dump 2026-06-12) and nothing detected it, because a plausible-looking 1.4GB .gz sat on disk the whole time. 'The backup file exists' is not 'the backup works' — only its FRESHNESS discriminates.\n\nLIKELY CAUSES (check in this order):\n 1. db-backup.sh's timer stopped firing: systemctl --user status papercusp-db-backup.timer papercusp-db-backup.service ; journalctl --user -u papercusp-db-backup.service -n 50. Read /mnt/backup/db-dumps/STATUS.json (ok / failed[] / lastSuccessEpoch) and /mnt/backup/db-dumps/db-backup.log.\n 2. A specific DB's dump is failing validation: db-backup.sh only renames a dump into place after pg_restore -l passes, so a per-DB 'FAIL' in db-backup.log means the archive was unreadable or the dump errored — read the actual pg_dump error there. DO NOT blindly raise PC_BACKUP_TIMEOUT_SEC without reading it.\n 3. An orphaned pg_dump is holding locks (the EI-10730 knock-on): check for a pg_dump backend older than its timeout (SELECT pid, application_name, backend_start FROM pg_stat_activity WHERE application_name LIKE 'pg_dump%'). db-backup.sh tags its own dumps PGAPPNAME=pcbackup and reaps foreign ones; a lingering 'pg_dump%' backend is a legacy/manual dump.\n 4. Disk full on the backup volume: df -h /mnt/backup/db-dumps\n\nAn orphaned/stuck dump is not just a dead backup — it holds AccessShareLock on ~593 tables, which starves every migration's ACCESS EXCLUSIVE and can wedge the whole release pipeline (EI-10730).","state":"blocked","assignee":"su-ee668749-03e6-4c1f-85c5-2feabab4c9ab","takenAt":"2026-10-13T23:21:05.896Z","lastProgressAt":"2026-10-14T02:27:22.877Z","assignedBy":null,"createdBy":"su-loopback","severity":"critical","goalId":null,"tags":null,"sourcePlanSlug":null,"sourcePlanItemIds":null,"redundancy":null,"expectedCostCents":null,"parent":null,"payload":{"_ei":{"scope":"harness:papercusp","source":"engineer","severity":"critical","created_by":"su-loopback","signal_origin":"organic"},"tags":["backup","data-safety","host-health"],"_claimHold":true,"held_open_at":"2026-10-14T02:11:55.285Z","held_open_by":"su-ee668749-03e6-4c1f-85c5-2feabab4c9ab","dedupCoverage":{"lexical":"ok","degraded":false,"semantic":"ok"},"dedupCandidates":[{"id":"WI-10000840","method":"semantic-hard","similarity":0.9955995080232345},{"id":"WI-10000693","method":"semantic-hard","similarity":0.9954397082328867},{"id":"WI-6554700010009969","method":"semantic-hard","similarity":0.995130359836244},{"id":"WI-6554700010008518","method":"semantic-hard","similarity":0.9951237733772795},{"id":"WI-10007742","method":"semantic-hard","similarity":0.9950738842851421},{"id":"WI-10000692","method":"semantic-hard","similarity":0.9935734868049693}],"externalBlockers":[{"ref":"harness:papercusp:storage:mnt-backup:dump-headroom-ready","kind":"event","status":"active","summary":"Waiting for /mnt/backup to reach >=300 GiB free so the DB dump can complete without violating its 120 GiB reserve.","evidence":"Live host reported 169.7 GiB free, while latest transcript backup admission required ~179 GiB estimated output plus 120 GiB reserve. The client receives HTTP 502 (aimd decreases=1, effective=12 — the window policy itself is correct). gateway.ts retryBudgetExhausted (~6717) logs 'internal-retry deadline … → shed 429, free slot (wedge-prevention ladder)', so the log and the wire disagree. A 502 reads to callers as a gateway error rather than a retryable throttle with retry-after. Candidate source (UNVERIFIED): request-kernel.ts 'gateway request exhausted its attempt budget without a response' (GatewayRequestKernelError ~1258) mapped to 502; request-kernel.ts was changed today (8e2edbfa, P-003/P-004 of gateway-admission-congestion-only-2026-10-13, su-b70be62e) — not yet established whether this predates it. No existing test pins the terminal status for this scenario (the ~line 796 edge-throttle test sends the same shape but asserts no status). Repro: npm run test:file -- packages/operator-core/lib/inference-gateway/gateway.test.ts -t 'CONGESTION 429' and change its notEqual(status,200) to equal(status,429). Root cause (measured): the Claude lane's D-014 ttfbCapMs (gateway.ts ~7042: retryDeadlineAt - now) could go <= 0 between route selection and the attempt. request-kernel.ts clamped it to a 1ms TTFB, so the attempt aborted at once and surfaced as a 502 'upstream stall'. FIX: (1) request-kernel.ts: a spent cap (floor < 1) throws 'retry-budget-exhausted' (outcome shed, non-retryable) and dispatches nothing. The error status is lastRetriedStatus (the status of the response discarded for the reattempt/failover; null after an error-driven retry), default 429. (2) gateway.ts Claude catch: 529 overloaded_error if status is 529, else 429 rate_limit_error. Both send shapeTerminalThrottle headers (x-should-retry false, retries-exhausted count) plus ROUTED_ACCOUNT_HEADER. (3) Tests: gateway.test.ts CONGESTION 429 test (~3936) asserts 429, x-should-retry false, retries-exhausted, no stall wording. request-kernel.test.ts gains a 'spent retry budget' describe with 3 tests (shed before attempt 1 = 429 with no dispatch; 529 carried between attempts; positive-budget control). The first revision's 429-for-everything failed gateway.test.ts:3010 (the 529 ladder-exhaustion test got 429); the revision fixes that. VERIFIED: request-kernel.test.ts 115/115. Root cause (measured): the Claude lane's D-014 ttfbCapMs (gateway.ts ~7042: retryDeadlineAt - now) could go <= 0 between route selection and the attempt. request-kernel.ts clamped it to a 1ms TTFB, so the attempt aborted at once and surfaced as a 502 'upstream stall'. FIX: (1) request-kernel.ts: a spent cap (floor < 1) throws 'retry-budget-exhausted' (outcome shed, non-retryable) and dispatches nothing. The error status is lastRetriedStatus (the status of the response discarded for the reattempt/failover; null after an error-driven retry), default 429. (2) gateway.ts Claude catch: 529 overloaded_error if status is 529, else 429 rate_limit_error. Both send shapeTerminalThrottle headers (x-should-retry false, retries-exhausted count) plus ROUTED_ACCOUNT_HEADER. (3) Tests: gateway.test.ts CONGESTION 429 test (~3936) asserts 429, x-should-retry false, retries-exhausted, no stall wording. request-kernel.test.ts gains a 'spent retry budget' describe with 3 tests (shed before attempt 1 = 429 with no dispatch; 529 carried between attempts; positive-budget control). The first revision's 429-for-everything failed gateway.test.ts:3010 (the 529 ladder-exhaustion test got 429); the revision fixes that. VERIFIED: request-kernel.test.ts 115/115. script and data in .papercusp/scratch/su-98253-sudo-lag/).\n\nOBSERVED: sudo pid 473367 was alive at 04:53:55Z (systemd: 'Unit process 473367 (sudo) remains running after unit stopped') but its COMMAND line has _SOURCE_REALTIME 04:54:32.875Z and journald receive 04:54:32.878Z. Host load ~220-280, memory PSI full avg10 up to ~73 in the window.\n\nIMPACT: every privileged step on this host (pg_dump via sudo -u postgres, Kopia via sudo, backup cleanups) inherits this latency. Added real Node:test suite registered in test:config and isolated native acceptance test.\n\n## Key insight\nDocumented npm test:file refused the newly untracked CommonJS test through committed-source-loader; the maintained desktop Node runner with monorepo-host-preflight passed 15 assertions before adding native acceptance. It failed pre-launch capacity admission: 84GB physical free minus 81GB active reservations leaves 3GB, cold target needs65GB.\n\n## Next action\nCheck maintained target-slot/capacity mechanism for an available warm native build slot; run config suite and native acceptance if capacity permits.\n\n---\n## Key insight\nSlow verification audit before a third attempt: two distinct preconditions failed, native Cargo root and then verifier /tmp source/profile/database storage. Both were correctly refused by independent preflights, but my first alternate-root attempt moved only Cargo while /tmp remained on the reserved data filesystem. Preserve evidence from both runs; no native/UI assertions ran. Durable repeatable invocation now selects a home-filesystem Cargo root AND TMPDIR, checks the combined native65GB + snapshot/profile16GB allocations against the shared reservation ledger before launching, and retains existing per-phase reservation checks. No capacity override or lock bypass.\n\n## Next action\nRun the registered isolated native test with both destinations on the measured home filesystem, after combined allocation admission.\n\n---\n## Did\nResume verification: zoom source, permission, package registration and native acceptance test are committed in desktop history (fcb443cc/1d473faa and package registration 7accd076). cargo check --offline for papercusp-desktop/src-tauri passed (1m37s); all9 zoom behavior/wiring tests pass. No zoom native assertions ran. Full Cargo compile check passed, proving the injection API/permission compile.
